	output reg [(GRANT_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] grantPtr;

      
	output reg [0:GRANT_NUM - 1] granted;

      
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);

      
	reg [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] reqTmp;

      
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		reqTmp = req;

      
		grant = 1'sb0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
			reg signed [31:0] p;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
				reg signed [31:0] _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
				for (p = 0; p < GRANT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
						granted[p] = 1'sb0;

      
						grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = 0;

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
							reg signed [31:0] e;

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
								reg signed [31:0] _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
								reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;

      
								_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;

      
								for (e = p; e < ENTRY_NUM; e = e + GRANT_NUM)

      
									if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
										if (reqTmp[e]) begin

      
											grant[e] = 1'sb1;

      
											granted[p] = 1'sb1;

      
											grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = e;

      
											reqTmp[e] = 1'sb0;

      
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
										end

      
										_sv2v_value_on_break = e;

      
									end

      
								if (!(_sv2v_jump < 2'b10))

      
									e = _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
								if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
									_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;

      
							end

      
						end

      
						_sv2v_value_on_break = p;

      
					end

      
				if (!(_sv2v_jump < 2'b10))

      
					p = _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
		end

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module CircularRangePicker (

      
	headPtr,

      
	tailPtr,

      
	request,

      
	grantPtr,

      
	picked

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 16;

      
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	input wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] request;

      
	output reg [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] grantPtr;

      
	output reg picked;

      
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);

      
	reg [(ENTRY_NUM * 2) - 1:0] rightShifterIn;

      
	wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] shiftedReq;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] shiftedGrant;

      
	generate

      
		if (ENTRY_NUM == 16) begin : genblk1

      
			CircularRangePickerRightShifter16 rightShifter(

      
				.shiftIn(rightShifterIn[30:0]),

      
				.shiftAmount(tailPtr),

      
				.shiftOut(shiftedReq)

      
			);

      
		end

      
		else begin : genblk1

      
			CircularRangePickerRightShifter #(

      
				.INPUT_WIDTH(ENTRY_NUM * 2),

      
				.SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH(INDEX_BIT_SIZE),

      
				.OUTPUT_WIDTH(ENTRY_NUM)

      
			) rightShifter(

      
				.shiftIn(rightShifterIn),

      
				.shiftAmount(tailPtr),

      
				.shiftOut(shiftedReq)

      
			);

      
		end

      
	endgenerate

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		rightShifterIn = {request, request};

      
		picked = 1'b0;

      
		shiftedGrant = 0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				if (shiftedReq[i]) begin

      
					shiftedGrant = i;

      
					picked = 1'b1;

      
				end

      
		end

      
		if ((shiftedGrant + tailPtr) < ENTRY_NUM)

      
			grantPtr = shiftedGrant + tailPtr;

      
		else

      
			grantPtr = (shiftedGrant + tailPtr) - ENTRY_NUM;

      
		if (tailPtr >= headPtr) begin

      
			if (((ENTRY_NUM + headPtr) - tailPtr) > shiftedGrant)

      
				picked = 1'b0;

      
		end

      
		else if ((headPtr - tailPtr) > shiftedGrant)

      
			picked = 1'b0;

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module CircularRangePickerRightShifter16 (

      
	shiftIn,

      
	shiftAmount,

      
	shiftOut

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	input wire [30:0] shiftIn;

      
	input wire [3:0] shiftAmount;

      
	output reg [15:0] shiftOut;

      
	reg [31:0] shiftTmp;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		shiftTmp = shiftIn;

      
		case (shiftAmount[3:2])

      
			2'h3: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[30:12];

      
			2'h2: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[26:8];

      
			2'h1: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[22:4];

      
			default: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[18:0];

      
		endcase

      
		case (shiftAmount[1:0])

      
			2'h3: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[18:3];

      
			2'h2: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[17:2];

      
			2'h1: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[16:1];

      
			default: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[15:0];

      
		endcase

      
		shiftOut = shiftTmp;

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module CircularRangePickerRightShifter (

      
	shiftIn,

      
	shiftAmount,

      
	shiftOut

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	parameter INPUT_WIDTH = 32;

      
	parameter SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH = 4;

      
	parameter OUTPUT_WIDTH = 16;

      
	input wire [INPUT_WIDTH - 1:0] shiftIn;

      
	input wire [SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH - 1:0] shiftAmount;

      
	output reg [OUTPUT_WIDTH - 1:0] shiftOut;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		shiftOut = shiftIn >> shiftAmount;

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module Multiplier (

      
	srcA,

      
	srcB,

      
	dst

      
);

      
	parameter BIT_WIDTH = 32;

      
	input wire signed [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcA;

      
	input wire signed [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcB;

      
	output wire signed [(2 * BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dst;

      
	assign dst = srcA * srcB;

      
endmodule

      
module SignExtender (

      
	in,

      
	sign,

      
	out

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	parameter BIT_WIDTH = 32;

      
	input wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] in;

      
	input wire sign;

      
	output reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] out;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		if (sign)

      
			out = {in[BIT_WIDTH - 1], in};

      
		else

      
			out = {1'b0, in};

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module PipelinedMultiplier (

      
	clk,

      
	stall,

      
	srcA,

      
	srcB,

      
	signA,

      
	signB,

      
	dst

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	parameter BIT_WIDTH = 32;

      
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire stall;

      
	input wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcA;

      
	input wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcB;

      
	input wire signA;

      
	input wire signB;

      
	output reg [(2 * BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dst;

      
	localparam RESULT_PIPELINE_DEPTH = PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2;

      
	reg signed [(2 * BIT_WIDTH) + 1:0] pipeReg [0:RESULT_PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1];

      
	reg signed [(2 * BIT_WIDTH) + 1:0] mulResult;

      
	wire signed [BIT_WIDTH:0] exSrcA;

      
	wire signed [BIT_WIDTH:0] exSrcB;

      
	reg signed [BIT_WIDTH:0] exSrcA_Reg;

      
	reg signed [BIT_WIDTH:0] exSrcB_Reg;

      
	SignExtender #(.BIT_WIDTH(BIT_WIDTH)) signExtenderA(

      
		.in(srcA),

      
		.sign(signA),

      
		.out(exSrcA)

      
	);

      
	SignExtender #(.BIT_WIDTH(BIT_WIDTH)) signExtenderB(

      
		.in(srcB),

      
		.sign(signB),

      
		.out(exSrcB)

      
	);

      
	generate

      
		if (!(PIPELINE_DEPTH > 2)) begin : genblk1

      
			RSD_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED non_existing_module();

      
		end

      
	endgenerate

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		(* use_dsp48 = "yes" *)

      
		mulResult = exSrcA_Reg * exSrcB_Reg;

      
		dst = pipeReg[RESULT_PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1][(2 * BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0];

      
	end

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (stall) begin

      
			exSrcA_Reg <= exSrcA_Reg;

      
			exSrcB_Reg <= exSrcB_Reg;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < RESULT_PIPELINE_DEPTH; i = i + 1)

      
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i];

      
			end

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			exSrcA_Reg <= exSrcA;

      
			exSrcB_Reg <= exSrcB;

      
			pipeReg[0] <= mulResult;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 1; i < RESULT_PIPELINE_DEPTH; i = i + 1)

      
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i - 1];

      
			end

      
		end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module Divider (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	req,

      
	dividend,

      
	divisor,

      
	isSigned,

      
	finished,

      
	quotient,

      
	remainder

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire req;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;

      
	input wire [31:0] dividend;

      
	input wire [31:0] divisor;

      
	input wire isSigned;

      
	output reg finished;

      
	output reg [31:0] quotient;

      
	output reg [31:0] remainder;

      
	parameter LOOP_COUNT = 33;

      
	parameter DIVIDER_WIDTH = LOOP_COUNT + 1;

      
	parameter DATA_MINUS_ONE = 34'sd4294967296 - 1;

      
	parameter DATA_MINIMUM = 33'sd2147483648;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regZ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextZ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regD;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextD;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regQ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextQ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regR;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextR;

      
	reg regSigned;

      
	reg nextSigned;

      
	reg [$clog2(LOOP_COUNT + 1) - 1:0] regCounter;

      
	reg [$clog2(LOOP_COUNT + 1) - 1:0] nextCounter;

      
	reg [1:0] regPhase;

      
	reg [1:0] nextPhase;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regZ <= 0;

      
			regD <= 0;

      
			regQ <= 0;

      
			regR <= 0;

      
			regPhase <= 2'd0;

      
			regCounter <= 0;

      
			regSigned <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regZ <= nextZ;

      
			regD <= nextD;

      
			regQ <= nextQ;

      
			regR <= nextR;

      
			regPhase <= nextPhase;

      
			regCounter <= nextCounter;

      
			regSigned <= nextSigned;

      
		end

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		finished = (regPhase == 2'd0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		if (req) begin

      
			if (isSigned) begin

      
				nextZ = (dividend[31] ? {2'b11, dividend} : {2'b00, dividend});

      
				nextD = (divisor[31] ? {2'b11, divisor} : {2'b00, divisor});

      
			end

      
			else begin

      
				nextZ = {2'b00, dividend};

      
				nextD = {2'b00, divisor};

      
			end

      
			nextQ = 0;

      
			nextR = (nextZ[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] ? -1 : 0);

      
			nextSigned = isSigned;

      
			nextPhase = 2'd1;

      
			nextCounter = 0;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			nextZ = regZ;

      
			nextD = regD;

      
			nextQ = regQ;

      
			nextR = regR;

      
			nextSigned = regSigned;

      
			nextCounter = regCounter;

      
			if (regPhase == 2'd1) begin

      
				nextR = (regR << 1) | regZ[(LOOP_COUNT - 1) - regCounter];

      
				if (nextR[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != regD[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1]) begin

      
					nextR = nextR + regD;

      
					nextQ = regQ << 1;

      
				end

      
				else begin

      
					nextR = nextR - regD;

      
					nextQ = (regQ << 1) | 1;

      
				end

      
				nextCounter = regCounter + 1;

      
				nextPhase = (nextCounter >= LOOP_COUNT ? 2'd2 : 2'd1);

      
			end

      
			else if (regPhase == 2'd2) begin

      
				nextQ = (regQ << 1) + 1;

      
				nextR = regR;

      
				if (regR[32:0] == 0)

      
					;

      
				else begin

      
					if (regR[32:0] == regD[32:0]) begin

      
						nextR = nextR - regD;

      
						nextQ = nextQ + 1;

      
					end

      
					else if ((regR[32:0] + regD[32:0]) == 0) begin

      
						nextR = nextR + regD;

      
						nextQ = nextQ - 1;

      
					end

      
					else if (regZ[32] != regR[32]) begin

      
						if (regR[32] != regD[32]) begin

      
							nextR = nextR + regD;

      
							nextQ = nextQ - 1;

      
						end

      
						else begin

      
							nextR = nextR - regD;

      
							nextQ = nextQ + 1;

      
						end

      
					end

      
					if (regD == 0) begin

      
						nextQ = -1;

      
						nextR = regZ;

      
					end

      
					else if ((regSigned && (regZ == DATA_MINIMUM)) && (regD == DATA_MINUS_ONE)) begin

      
						nextQ = regZ;

      
						nextR = 0;

      
					end

      
				end

      
				nextPhase = 2'd0;

      
			end

      
			else

      
				nextPhase = regPhase;

      
		end

      
		quotient = regQ;

      
		remainder = regR;

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule

      
module QuickDivider (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	req,

      
	dividend,

      
	divisor,

      
	isSigned,

      
	finished,

      
	quotient,

      
	remainder

      
);

      
	reg _sv2v_0;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire req;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;

      
	input wire [31:0] dividend;

      
	input wire [31:0] divisor;

      
	input wire isSigned;

      
	output reg finished;

      
	output reg [31:0] quotient;

      
	output reg [31:0] remainder;

      
	parameter DIVIDER_WIDTH = 33;

      
	function automatic [$clog2(DIVIDER_WIDTH + 1) - 1:0] CountSignificantBits;

      
		input reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] dividend;

      
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
		begin

      
			_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
			CountSignificantBits = 1;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
					reg signed [31:0] _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
					for (i = DIVIDER_WIDTH - 2; i >= 0; i = i - 1)

      
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
							if (dividend[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != dividend[i]) begin

      
								CountSignificantBits = i + 2;

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
							end

      
							_sv2v_value_on_break = i;

      
						end

      
					if (!(_sv2v_jump < 2'b10))

      
						i = _sv2v_value_on_break;

      
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
				end

      
			end

      
		end

      
	endfunction

      
	parameter MAX_LOOP_COUNT = 33;

      
	parameter DATA_MINUS_ONE = 34'sd4294967296 - 1;

      
	parameter DATA_MINIMUM = 33'sd2147483648;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regZ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextZ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regD;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextD;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regQ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextQ;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] regR;

      
	reg [DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1:0] nextR;

      
	reg regSigned;

      
	reg nextSigned;

      
	reg [$clog2(MAX_LOOP_COUNT + 1) - 1:0] regCounter;

      
	reg [$clog2(MAX_LOOP_COUNT + 1) - 1:0] nextCounter;

      
	reg [1:0] regPhase;

      
	reg [1:0] nextPhase;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regZ <= 0;

      
			regD <= 0;

      
			regQ <= 0;

      
			regR <= 0;

      
			regPhase <= 2'd0;

      
			regCounter <= 0;

      
			regSigned <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regZ <= nextZ;

      
			regD <= nextD;

      
			regQ <= nextQ;

      
			regR <= nextR;

      
			regPhase <= nextPhase;

      
			regCounter <= nextCounter;

      
			regSigned <= nextSigned;

      
		end

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (_sv2v_0)

      
			;

      
		finished = (regPhase == 2'd0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		if (req) begin

      
			if (isSigned) begin

      
				nextZ = (dividend[31] ? {1'b1, dividend} : {1'b0, dividend});

      
				nextD = (divisor[31] ? {1'b1, divisor} : {1'b0, divisor});

      
			end

      
			else begin

      
				nextZ = {1'b0, dividend};

      
				nextD = {1'b0, divisor};

      
			end

      
			nextQ = 0;

      
			nextR = (nextZ[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] ? -1 : 0);

      
			nextSigned = isSigned;

      
			nextPhase = 2'd1;

      
			nextCounter = CountSignificantBits(nextZ);

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			nextZ = regZ;

      
			nextD = regD;

      
			nextQ = regQ;

      
			nextR = regR;

      
			nextSigned = regSigned;

      
			nextCounter = regCounter;

      
			if (regPhase == 2'd1) begin

      
				nextR = (regR << 1) | regZ[regCounter - 1];

      
				if (regR[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != regD[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1]) begin

      
					nextR = nextR + regD;

      
					nextQ = -1;

      
				end

      
				else begin

      
					nextR = nextR - regD;

      
					nextQ = 0;

      
				end

      
				nextCounter = regCounter - 1;

      
				nextPhase = (nextCounter == 0 ? 2'd3 : 2'd2);

      
			end

      
			else if (regPhase == 2'd2) begin

      
				nextR = (regR << 1) | regZ[regCounter - 1];

      
				if (regR[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != regD[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1]) begin

      
					nextR = nextR + regD;

      
					nextQ = regQ << 1;

      
				end

      
				else begin

      
					nextR = nextR - regD;

      
					nextQ = (regQ << 1) | 1;

      
				end

      
				nextCounter = regCounter - 1;

      
				nextPhase = (nextCounter == 0 ? 2'd3 : 2'd2);

      
			end

      
			else if (regPhase == 2'd3) begin

      
				nextQ = (regQ << 1) + 1;

      
				nextR = regR;

      
				if (regR == 0)

      
					;

      
				else if (regR == regD) begin

      
					nextR = nextR - regD;

      
					nextQ = nextQ + 1;

      
				end

      
				else if (regR == -regD) begin

      
					nextR = nextR + regD;

      
					nextQ = nextQ - 1;

      
				end

      
				else if (regR[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != regZ[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1]) begin

      
					if (regR[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1] != regD[DIVIDER_WIDTH - 1]) begin

      
						nextR = nextR + regD;

      
						nextQ = nextQ - 1;

      
					end

      
					else begin

      
						nextR = nextR - regD;

      
						nextQ = nextQ + 1;

      
					end

      
				end

      
				if (regD == 0) begin

      
					nextQ = -1;

      
					nextR = regZ;

      
				end

      
				else if ((regSigned && (regZ == DATA_MINIMUM)) && (regD == DATA_MINUS_ONE)) begin

      
					nextQ = regZ;

      
					nextR = 0;

      
				end

      
				nextPhase = 2'd0;

      
			end

      
			else

      
				nextPhase = regPhase;

      
		end

      
		quotient = regQ;

      
		remainder = regR;

      
	end

      
	initial _sv2v_0 = 0;

      
endmodule
